iṉṉa uvamattukku iṉṉa vāypāṭu uriya eṉpatu
varalāṟṟu muṟiyāṉ aṟiyappaṭumāṟu
292.	tatta marapiṉ tōṉṟumaṉ poruḷē.
itu, nāṉku uvamattukkum orōvoṉṟu eṭṭākak kūṟiya vāy pāṭṭiṟkellām puṟaṉaṭi.
(i-ḷ.) iṉṉataṟku iṉṉa vāypāṭu uriyaveṉṟaṟkuk kāraṇam eṉṉiyeṉṟārkku, avi tattam varalāṟṟu muṟimiyāṉē avvap poruṭōṉṟa niṟkum e-ṟu.
"pulipāyntāṅkup pāyntā'ṉeṉa viṉiyuvamattiṟku vanta āṅkeṉ kiḷavi 'taḷirāṅkuc civantamēṉi' yeṉiṟ poruntātu.
iṉi, eḷḷaveṉpatu payaṉuvamattiṟku ēṟkumeṉṟāṉ; atu 'puli yeḷḷum pāyttu'ḷeṉalākātu; eṉṉi? pulikku vali kūṟiṉaṉṟi ataṉōṭu uvamikkappaṭṭa cāttaṟkup pukaḻākātākalā ṉeṉpatu. oruvaṉi veṉṟi kūṟuṅkāl avaṉōṭu oppikkiṉṟa puliyēṟṟiṉi avaṉik kaṇṭu etirniṟkalāṟṟātu puṟaṅkoṭuttateṉalum, naṭuṅkiṟ ṟeṉaluṅ, kaṇ cimpuḷittateṉalum, avaṟkup pukaḻāmeṉpatē karutik kūṟiṉ, kūṟiṉa uvama veḷippāṭumiṉṟi avaṟkatu pukaḻumākāteṉpatu karuttu.
iṉi, maḻiyiṉik koṭikku iḻittuc collavum peṟupa vaṉṟē? avvāṟu colliṉum ataṉi avaṉiṉ iḻittu nōkkātu ulakamātaliṉ.
viḻiya vīḻa pulla matippa vella eṉpaṉavum uvamitāṉ poruḷi yottaṟ kavāviṉaveṉṟu poruḷ tōṉṟa niṟkumeṉṟa vāṟu. kaḷḷaveṉpatūum ataṉ kuṇam ataṉkaṇ illāmaṟ katuviṟṟeṉṉum poruṭṭu. poruvaveṉpatūum maḻiyiṉiyum oruvaṉiyum uṟaḻun tuṇic ciṟantāṉeṉac collutal. ivvāṟṟāṉ ivi urimi kūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
iṉi, meyyuvamattiṟku uriyaveṉappaṭṭaṉavum avvāṟē oru kāraṇamuṭiya pōlum. kaṭuppa eṉṟakkāl viṉikkum payattiṟkum ēlātu; eṉṉi? kaṭuttaleṉpatu aiyuṟutal. puliyoṭu maṟavaṉi aiyuṟavēṇṭuva toru kāraṇamiṉmiyāṉum, maḻiyiṉ viḷitta payattōṭu uvamittaliṉ maḻiyōṭu oruvaṉi aiyañceytal vēṇṭuvatiṉmiyāṉum, vaṭivukaṇṭavaḻi aiyam piṟakkumākalāṉum, uruveṉpatu kuṇamākalāṟ poruḷvēṟṟumi aṟintavaḻi aiyañ cellātākalāṉum, meykkurimi kūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu. ēyttaleṉpa tūum poruntutalākaliṉ vaṭiviṟkēṟkum. maruḷa puriya oṭṭa oṭuṅka eṉpaṉavuṅ kaṭuttalpōlum poruṇmiya; eṉṉi? maruṭciyum puriyuṇarvuṅ kavarttalik kāṭṭutalāṉum, oṭṭa oṭuṅka eṉpaṉavum iraṇṭaṉiyum oṉṟeṉṉum poruṇmiyavākalāṉu meṉpatu, nikarttalum avviṉap poruḷeṉpataṉik kāṭṭutaliṉ vaṭiviṟ kēṟṟatu. ōṭaveṉpatum ōṭutaṟṟoḻil vaṭiviṟ kallatiṉmiyiṉ av vaṭiviṟkēṟṟatu. paṇpāyiṟ paṇpu niṟappaṇpu, ōṭiṟṟeṉalākāmiyiṉ.
iṉi, uruvuvama vāypāṭṭiṟkuṅ kāraṇaṅ kūṟuṅkāṟ pōlu meṉpatu iṭiccollākalāṉum, marīivanta viṉippāṟ paṭṭatāka lāṉum, ataṟkuk kāraṇaṅ kūṟappaṭāteṉpatu. aḵtēl, ataṉi ivveṭṭaṟkum muṉpu kūṟiyateṉṉi, poruḷuṭiyavaṟṟip piṟkūṟi yeṉiṉ, atuvum aṉṉa veṉpatupōla maṟṟi mūṉṟu uvamattum payiṉṟuvarumeṉpatu eytuvit taṟkeṉpatu. maṟuppa oppa eṉpaṉa mutalāyiṉavum oru kāraṇamuṭiya veṉpatu āciriyaṉ peruvaraviṉa vāka urimippaṭuttuk kūṟiṉamiyiṉ aṟintām. allatūum 'marapiṟ ṟōṉṟum' eṉṟataṉāṉ iviyellām marapupaṟṟi aṟiyal vēṇṭum eṉavē, taliccaṅkattār mutalāyiṉār ceyyuḷkaḷuḷ avvāṟu payiṉṟu varumeṉpatu aṟintāmaṉṟē, ivvāṟu cūttirañ ceytalā ṉeṉpatu.	(17)
uvamam eṭṭākum eṉal
293.	nāliraṇ ṭākum pālumā ruṇṭē
ituvum mēlaṉavaṟṟiyē pakukkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) viṉi payaṉ mey uru eṉappaṭṭa nāṉkum eṭṭām pakutiyum uṇṭu e-ṟu.
avi uvamattoki nāṉkum uvamaviri nāṉkumeṉa eṭṭātalum uṭiyaveṉṟavāṟu. avi puliyaṉṉa pāyttuḷ 'pulippāyttuḷ' eṉavum, maḻiyaṉṉa vaṇki 'maḻi vaṇki' eṉavum, vēyaṉṉatōḷ 'vēyt tōḷ' eṉavum, pavaḷattaṉṉavāy 'pavaḷavāy' eṉavum tokiviripaṟṟi nāṉkum eṭṭāyiṉavāṟu. collōttiṉuḷ ivvāypāṭu virintu varumāṟu kūṟātu āṇṭut tokiyārāyccippaṭṭa māttiriyāṉē kūṟiṉāṉ. avvuvamantāṉ īṇṭuk kūṟappaṭutal poruḷiṉatākalāṉum, iṭiccollēyaṉṟip poruḷ payappaṉavum avvurupākalāṉum, ataṉ viriviṉi īṇṭup peyartantu kūṟi ataṉ tokiyoṭu paṭuppa ittuṇip pakutiyavām annālvaki yuvamamum eṉṟāṉeṉpatu.
ituvumoru karuttu: muṉṉar evveṭṭākak kūṟiyavi ovvoṉṟum ivviraṇṭu kūṟāki eṭṭām pakutiyuṭiya eṉṟavāṟu. yāṅkaṉameṉiṉ, aṉṉa eṉṉuñ colmutalākiya eṭṭaṉuḷ, aṉṉa āṅka māṉa eṉṉa eṉappaṭṭa nāṉkum vēṟoru poruḷi uṇarttā miyiṉ ōriṉamāki oṉṟākavum, viṟappa uṟaḻa takiya nōkka eṉṉum nāṉkum oru poruḷuṭimiyiṉ ōriṉamākavum, ivvāṟē iṉam nōkkutaṟ kuṟippiṉa vāyiṉa. ivvāṟṟāṉ iraṇṭeṉavum paṭum eṭṭumeṉṟavāṟu. itaṉatu payaṉ ivviraṇṭu kūṟṟāṉ aṭakkappaṭum viṉiyuvamaccol eṭṭum (287) eṉṟavāṟu. viṟattal, iṉamākac ceṟiyumeṉṉum poruṭṭu. uṟaḻcciyun taṉ iṉamākak koṇṭu - māṟutaṟ poruṭṭēyām. takuti atu veṉappaṭuvatu eṉṉum poruṇmittākaliṉ avaṟṟōṭokkum. nōkkeṉpatūum avvāṟē iṉamākki nōkkutaṟporuṭṭu. ivvāṟṟāṉ iraṇ ṭeṉavumpaṭum eṭṭu meṉṟavāṟu. itaṉatu payaṉ:-ōtiya vāypāṭu eṇ ṇāṉkaṟkum iṉṉa vāypāṭum iṉṉa vāypāṭum oru poruḷaveṉṟu aṟitalum avi tattam marapiṟ poruḷtōṉṟa varum iṭiccol eṉṟalum, oppil vaḻiyāṟ poruḷ ceyyum urupu āyiṉum iṭic collākā, teriniliviṉi ākum eṉpataṟivittalumeṉakkoḷka. mēlvarukiṉṟaṉavaṟṟiṟkum iḵtokkumeṉpatu.
iṉip, payavuvama vāypāṭu eṭṭaṉuḷḷum eḷḷa poruva kaḷḷa vella eṉṉum nāṉkum uvamattiṉi yiḻittaṟ poruḷavākki oṉṟā yaṭaṅkum; eṉṉi? maḻiyip porīic collutalum ataṉatu taṉmikkuṇaṅ kaḷḷappaṭutalum ataṉi velkiyum eḷḷutalum pōlvaṉa iḻiviṉiyē kāṭṭutaliṉ. iṉi viḻiya pulla matippa vīḻa eṉṉum nāṉkum uvamikkappaṭum poruḷiṉi uyarttāmiyāṉum uvamattiṉi iḻittuk kūṟāmiyāṉum avi nāṉkum oru poruḷ eṉappaṭṭaṉa. iv vāṟṟāṟ payavuvamam eṭṭum (289) iraṇṭāyiṉa veṉpatu.
meyyuvamam iraṇṭāṅkāl,aiyapporuṭkaṇ nāṉkun tuṇi poruṭkaṇ nāṉkumeṉa iraṇṭām. kaṭuppa maruḷa puriya ōṭa eṉṉum nāṉkum aiyapporuḷavāki oṉṟām. ōṭaveṉpatu uvamattiṉkaṇṇum poruḷiṉkaṇṇum uṇarvu kavarntōṭiṟṟeṉṉum poruḷ tōṉṟac collaliṉ atuvum aiyameṉappaṭṭatu pōlum. iṉi ēyppa oṭṭa oṭuṅka nikarppa eṉṉum nāṉkum aiyamiṉṟi uvamamum poruḷum oṉṟeṉa uṇarvutōṉṟum vāypāṭākaliṉ ivi nāṉkum oṉṟeṉappaṭṭu iviyeṭṭum (290) iraṇṭāyiṉa.
iṉi, uruviṉ kaṇṇum (291) pōla oppa nēra naḷiya eṉṉum nāṉ kum maṟutaliyiṉṟic cērntaṉaveṉṟukōṭaṟku vāypāṭāki varutaliṉ avi oṉṟeṉappaṭṭaṉa.
'naḷiyeṉ kiḷavi ceṟivu mākum' (tol. col. uri. 17)) eṉṟata ṉāṉ, ataṉoṭu cērntateṉṉum poruṭṭēyāyiṟṟu. iṉi maṟuppa kāytta viyappa nanta eṉṉum nāṉkum uvamiyoṭu maṟutali tōṉṟi niṟkum poruḷavākaliṉ nāṉkum oṉṟeṉappaṭṭu iviyeṭṭum iraṇṭāyiṉa. nantutaleṉpatu kēṭu. viyattaleṉpatu uvamiyāṉ viyakkat takkatu poruḷeṉavē ataṉkaṇ akkuṇamiṉṟeṉa maṟuttavāṟām. kāyttaleṉpatūum uvamiyik kāyppittalākaliṉ atuvum maṟuttaleṉpataṉ poruḷeṉappaṭṭatu.
ivvāṟu iviyellān tokuppa eṭṭātalum uṇṭeṉpatu itaṉ karuttu. ivaṟṟuṭ palavaṟṟiyuñ ceyaveṉecca vāypāṭṭāṉ ōtiyatu eṉṉiyeṉiṉ, aḵtu(665)uṭampoṭu puṇarttaleṉpataṉāṉ. iṭiccollum uriccollum viṉiccoṟpōla niṟkumeṉavum, ataṉāṉē peyareccamum viṉiyeccamum muṟṟumāki niṟkumeṉavum, aviyun teriniliviṉiyuvamamāy varumeṉavum aṟivittaṟkeṉka. ivaṟṟi ivvāṟu eṭṭākac collutal peritum nuṇṇuṇarviṟ ṟeṉavuṇarka.	(18)
meyppāṭu eṭṭaṉvaḻip perumiyum ciṟumiyum paṟṟi
uvamam tōṟṟutal
294.	perumiyuñ ciṟumiyu meyppāṭu eṭṭaṉ
	vaḻimaruṅku aṟiyat tōṉṟu meṉpa.
itu, mēṟkūṟiya uvamam iṉṉumoruvāṟṟāṉ eṭṭeṉappaṭu meṉpatu uṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) perumipaṟṟiyuñ ciṟumipaṟṟiyum oppumi koḷḷap paṭum uvamam mēṟkūṟiya meyppāṭeṭṭaṉvaḻit tōṉṟumeṉṟu colluvar pulavar e-ṟu.
meyppāṭu eṭṭeṉpaṉa:
'nakiyē yaḻuki yiḷivaral maruṭki
yaccam perumitam vekuḷi yuvaki'	(tol poruḷ. 21)
eṉa meyppāṭṭiyaluḷ mēṟkūṟappaṭṭaṉa.
"kaḷavuṭam paṭunariṟ kaviḻntu nilaṅkiḷiyā"	(akam. 16)
eṉpatu nakiyuvamam. eṉṉi? talimakaṉik kaṇṭaṟiyātāḷ pōlak karantoḻukukiṉṟa paratti avaṉōṭu oppumi kaṇṭu taṉiniṉṟu viḷi yāṭum putalvaṉik koṇṭu makiḻkiṉṟāḷi, vāyiṟkatavam maṟintu niṉṟa talimakaḷ 'nīyum ammakaviṟkut tāyēkāṇ' eṉṟavaḻik, kaḷavu kaṇṭa poruḷoṭu kiyakappaṭṭa kaḷvarpōlac ceyvataṟiyātu taṭumāṟip paratti mukam vēṟupaṭṭa nilimiyi uvamittuc cirittamiyiṉ nakiyuvama māyiṟṟu.
"kalaṅkaviḻnta nāykaṉpōṟ kaḷituṇi piṟitiṉṟip
pulampumeṉ ṉilikaṇṭum pōkalaṉē yeṉṟiyāl"	(yā. vi. mēṟ.)
eṉpatu avalavuvamam. kalaṅkaviḻnta nīkāmaṉ pōlap pulampiṉāḷeṉṟa miyiṉ appeyarttāyiṟṟu.
"peruñcelva rillattu nalkūrntār pōla
varuñcellum pērumeṉ ṉeñcu"	(muttoḷ. 88)
eṉpatu, iḷivaraluvamam; eṉṉi? talimakaṉmāṭṭu iṉpa viḷiyāṭ ṭeytuvār palariyuṅ kaṇṭu neñcu tīyap puṉkaṇeytit taṉiniṉṟu pukap peṟātu iḷivantamiyiṉ appeyarttāyiṟṟu.
"īrattu ḷiṉṉavi tōṉṟi ṉiḻaṟkayattuḷ
nīruṭ kuvaḷiven taṟṟu"	(kali. 41)
eṉpatu maruṭki yuvamam; eṉṉi? niḻaṟkayattiṉ kuvaḷi vēvaṉa viṉmiyiṉ iḵtu aṟputamāyiṟṟu.
"cāntakat tuṇṭeṉṟu cepput tiṟantoruvaṉ
pāmpakattuk kaṇṭa tuṭittu"	(nālaṭi. 126)
eṉpatu accamākaliṉ accavuvamam.
"mallari maṟañcāytta mālpōṟṟaṉ kiḷināppaṇ
kalluyar naṉañcāraṟ kalantiyalu nāṭakēḷ"	(kali. 52)
eṉpatu perumitavuvamam.
"kūṟṟuvekuṇ ṭaṉṉa muṉpoṭu
māṟṟiru vēntar maṇṇōk kiṉiyē"	(puṟam. 42)
eṉpatu vekuḷiyuvamam.
"pāṭic ceṉṟa paricilar pōla
vuvaviṉi vāḻi tōḻi"	(akam. 65)
eṉpatu uvakiyuvamam piṟavumaṉṉa.
ivaṟṟāṉ eṇvaki meyppāṭum paṟṟi uvamam eṭṭeṉappaṭu meṉṟu aṟintu kūṟiṉāṉ;
"perumiyuñ ciṟumiyuñ ciṟappiṟ ṟīrā"	(poruḷ. 285)
eṉpuḻip peṟṟāmākaliṉ. meyppāṭeṭṭaṉvaḻit tōṉṟumeṉ ṉātu 'maruṅkaṟiya' veṉṟataṉāṟ perumiyuñ ciṟumiyum paṟṟi varutal vaḻimaruṅkeṉavum avipaṟṟātu meyppāṭu eṭṭumpaṟṟi vāḷātē uvamam varutal cevviteṉavuṅ koḷḷappaṭum.
"nīruṭ kuvaḷiven taṟṟu" (kali. 41) eṉpatu, perumi paṟṟiyatu. eṉṉi? ulakanaṭi yiṟantatōr uvamam kūṟi ataṉoṭu talimakaṉ īrattiṉi oppittamiyiṉ.
"kaḷavuṭam paṭunariṟ kaviḻntunilaṅ kiḷiyā"	(akam. 16)
eṉpatu, taṉakku nikarāmal iḻitturittamiyiṟ ciṟumipaṟṟi vantatām.
uvamamum meyppāṭum poruḷkaḷi aṟivippaṉavāki avi vēṟuvēṟu poruḷ aṟivittaliṉ ōttu vēṟupāṭu uṭiyaṉavāyiṉuñ ciṟupāṉmi mayaṅkiyum varumeṉṟaṟku itu kūṟavēṇṭiyateṉpatu.(19)
uvamattāṉ poruṭku uṟṟaṉa aṟintu tuṇital
295.	uvamap poruḷiṉ uṟṟatu uṇarun
	teḷimaruṅku uḷavē tiṟattiya lāṉa.
iḵtu, eytātateytuvittal nutaliṟṟu; uvamattoṭu poruḷ ovvāteṉavum oppumi cārttik koḷḷumāṟuṇarttiṉamiyiṉ.
(i-ḷ.) uvamapporuḷiṉ. - uvamam eṉappaṭṭa poruḷāṉ; iṉ urupu āṉ urupiṉkaṇ vantatu; uṟṟa tuṇarun teḷimaruṅkuḷa  - uvamikka varum poruṭ kuṟṟatellām aṟintu tuṇiyum pakutiyumuḷa; tiṟattiya lāṉa - aṅṅaṉam tuṇiyappaṭum poruṭṭiṟam palavākivarum ilakkaṇa vakiyāṉ e-ṟu.
appakuti palavum uṟṟuṇarāmaṟ colliyavaḻiyum, aḵtu uṇara varumeṉpatu karuttu. 'tiṟattiyalāṉa' eṉappaṭṭa pakutiyāvaṉa: mēṟkūṟap paṭṭa meyppāṭeṭṭum paṟṟi uvamaṅkoḷḷuṅkāl, 'uṟṟatuṇarun teḷi maruṅ'keṉavē uvamāṉavaṭikku uvamēyavaṭi kuṟintu varuvaṉavum yātum aṭiyiṉṟi varuvaṉavumeṉṟu ivviraṇṭum uṟṟuṇarāmaṟ colliyavaḻi, avaṟṟukkum uvamapporuḷē teḷi maruṅkāmeṉavum, vāḷātē uvamañceytu uṟṟatuṇarttātavaḻiyum atuvē teḷimaruṅkā meṉavum iṉṉōraṉṉa koḷka. utāraṇam:
"kaḷavuṭam paṭunariṟ kaviḻntu nilaṅ kiḷiyā "	(akam. 16)
eṉṟavaḻik, kaṇṭōrkkellām perunakiyākak kaḷavuṇṭākap paṭunariṟ kaviḻntu nilaṅkiḷiyāveṉa uṟṟatuṇarak kūṟiyatila ṉāyiṉuṅ kiyoṭupaṭṭa kaḷavuṭiyārpōla niṉṟāḷeṉṉum uvamap poruḷāṉē eḷḷutaṟporuḷ tōṉṟi naki pulappaṭuvatāyiṟṟu.
"cāṟutalik koṇṭeṉap peṇṇīṟ ṟuṟṟeṉa
paṭṭa māri ñāṉṟa ñāyiṟṟuk
kaṭṭil niṇakku miḻiciṉaṉ kiyatu
pōḻtūṇ ṭūciyiṉ virintaṉṟu mātō
ūrkoḷa vanta porunaṉōṭu
ārpuṉi teriyal neṭuntaki pōrē"	(puṟam. 82)
eṉṉum pāṭṭiṉuḷ, uvamapporuḷākiya "pōḻtūṇṭūcikkup" pala aṭi kūṟi ataṉōṭu uvamikkappaṭum pōrttoḻiliṉi yātumōr aṭiyiṉṟi vāḷātu kūṟiṉāṉāyiṉum, uvamapporuḷāṉē pōrt toḻiṟ kuṟṟatum uṇarakkūṟiṉāṉām; eṉṉi? uṇṭāṭṭuṅ koṭiyum uraṉoṭu nōkki maṟuttalum mutalākiya uḷḷak karuttiṉāṉ oru kaṇattuḷḷē pala vēntari oruṅku vēṟaṟku virikiṉṟatu pōrttoḻileṉpatu teḷiyappaṭṭamiyiṉ.
"uḻuta nōṉpaka ṭaḻitiṉ ṟāṅku
nallamiḻ tākanī nayantuṇṇu naṟavē"	(puṟam. 125)
eṉapatum,
"maruntukoḷ marattiṉ vāḷvaṭu mayaṅki"	(puṟam. 180)
eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉavum avi.
iṉi, vāḷātē uvamañceytu uṟṟatuṇarttātavaḻit teḷiyu māṟu:
"uruvukaṇ ṭeḷḷāmi vēṇṭu muruḷperuntērk
kaccāṇi yaṉṉā ruṭittu"	(kuṟaḷ. 667)
eṉpataṉuḷ, avar ceykivaṉmi kūṟārāyiṉum accāṇiyeṉṟa uvamapporuḷ tāṉē acceykivaṉmi kūṟiṟṟu.
"vēṉiṟ puṉalaṉṉa nuntiyi nōvāryār"	(kali. 84)
eṉpatum atu. oḻintaṉavu maṉṉa.
iṉi. uvamattiṉ uṟṟatuṇarkaveṉṉātu 'poru' ḷeṉṟataṉāṟ poruṭku aṭutta aṭiyum uvama aṭikku ēṟṟatu uṇarappaṭātaṉa kaḷiyappaṭumeṉpatu. atu,
"pācaṭi nivanta kaṇikkā ṉeyta
liṉamī ṉiruṅkaḻi yōtam malkutoṟuṅ
kayamūḻku makaḷir kaṇṇiṉ māṉum"	(kuṟun. 9)
eṉṟavaḻip, pācaṭinivanta kaṇikkāleṉa neytalākiya poruṭku vanta aṭiyiraṇṭuṅ kaṇṇeṉappaṭṭa uvamattiṟkēṟpa vārāmiyāṉ avi teḷimaruṅkilaveṉṟu kaḷintu koḷka. oḻintaṉavu maṉṉa.
maṟṟup,
"puṟamati yaṉṉa tirumuka miṟiñci"	
eṉṟavaḻi, akattut tōṉṟum maṟuppōlap puṟattumaṟu matikkilli yeṉpatūum puṟamatipōlak kaṟitīrnta mukameṉpatūum uvamap poruḷiṉ uṟṟatuṇarun teḷimaruṅ keṉṟataṉāṟ kōṭumōveṉiṉ, aṅṅaṉaṅ koḷḷāmikkaṉṟē varukiṉṟa cūttirameṉpatu.	(20)
uvamapporuḷ marapupaṟṟi aṟiyappaṭum eṉal
296.	uvamap poruḷi uṇaruṅ kāli
	marīiya marapiṉ vaḻakkoṭu paṭumē.
itu, mēlataṟkoru puṟaṉaṭi; mēl uvamapporuḷāṉē uṟṟa tuṇarac ceyal vēṇṭumeṉṟāṉ; iṉi aṅṅaṉam uṇarumāṟu itu kūṟiṉāṉātaliṉ.
(i-ḷ.) uvamapporuḷāṉ uṟṟatuṇaruṅkāli marīi vanta vaḻak koṭupaṭuttu aṟiyappaṭum e-ṟu.
atu varumāṟu:
"kaḷavuṭam paṭunariṟ kaviḻntunilaṅ kiḷiyā"	(akam. 16)
eṉpatu kaḷavuṭampaṭunarkkuḷḷa vēṟupāṭu ulakattu aṭippaṭa vanta vaḻak kātalāṉ, aḵtu ētuvāka ataṉiyum aṟintu koḷḷappaṭumeṉṟavāṟu. eṉavē, ulakattu vaḻakkiṉum aṭippaṭat tōṉṟum uvamam ākāmi yāṉ uvamapporuḷ pulappāṭu ceyyātu,
"puṟamati pōlu mukam"	
eṉṟatu pōlvatu eṉpatu itaṉ karuttu.	(21)
aṭiyaṭutta poruṭku aṭiyaṭutta uvamamē kūṟavēṇṭutal
297.	iraṭṭik kiḷavi iraṭṭi vaḻittē.
iḵtu, eytātateytuvittatu, aṭiyoṭu vanta poruḷoṭu puṇarkkumāṟu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ.
(i-ḷ.) iraṭṭikkiḷavi - aṭiyum aṭiyaṭutta poruḷu meṉa iraṇṭākac collappaṭuṅ kiḷavi; iraṭṭi vaḻittē  - aṭiyum aṭi yaṭutta poruḷumeṉa iraṇṭākki niṟuttappaṭum uvamattiṉ vaḻittu e-ṟu.
"poṉkāṇ kaṭṭaḷi kaṭuppak kaṇpiṉ
puṉkāyc cuṇṇam puṭitta mārpiṉ" 	(perumpāṇ. 220)
eṉa varum.
taṉ pimpūṇ puṭitta ceñcuvaṭṭiṉiyum mārpiṉiyum poṉ ṉuriyōṭuṅ kallōṭum uvamittamiyiṉ, iraṭṭik kiḷavi iraṭṭi vaḻittāyiṟṟu. iraṭṭik kiḷaviyeṉap poruḷiṉi muṟkūṟiyataṉāṉ, iraṇṭu poruḷiṉi oṉṟākak kūṭṭi uvamikkak karutiṉāṉ uvamattiṉiyum iraṇṭu oṉṟākkiyē uvamikkumeṉpatu karuttu. vikārattāṉ tokku niṉṟa ummiyāṉ, oṟṟikkiḷaviyum iraṭṭi vaḻittāki varuvaṉa koḷka. atu,
"karuṅkāl vēṅki vīyuku tuṟuka
lirumpulik kuruḷiyiṟ ṟōṉṟuṅ kāṭṭiṭi"	(kuṟun. 47)
eṉa varum. irumpulikkuruḷi yoṉṟaṉiyē tuṟukalloṭum vēṅki vīyoṭum oppittamiyiṉ ippeyarttāyiṟṟu.	(22)
uḷḷuṟiyuvamam tōṉṟumāṟu
298.	piṟitoṭu paṭāatu piṟappoṭu nōkki
	muṉṉi marapiṟ kūṟuṅ kālit
	tuṇivoṭu varūun tuṇiviṉōr koḷiṉē.
itu mēlellām ēṉiyuvamaṅ kūṟi, uḷḷuṟiyuvamam uṇart tutal nutaliṟṟu. mēl 'icitirinticikku' (195) meṉappaṭṭavaṟṟiṉ pakutiyāyiṉum itaṉi āṇṭuk (196) kūṟātu īṇṭuk kūṟiṉāṉ uḷḷuṟi yuvamamākalāṉum ivvōttu uvamaviyalākalāṉumeṉpatu.
(i-ḷ.) piṟitoṭu paṭātu- uvamattoṭu uvamikkappaṭum poruḷ piṟitoṉṟu tārātu; piṟappoṭu nōkki- uvamanilaṅkaḷuṭ piṟanta piṟavikaḷoṭu cārtti nōkki; muṉṉi marapiṟ kūṟuṅkāli- karuttiṉāṉ  itaṟku iḵtu uvamameṉṟu coṉṉa marapiṉāṟ kūṟuṅ kāli; tuṇivoṭu varūum tuṇiviṉōr koḷiṉē- iṉṉa poruṭku iḵtu uvamamāyiṟ ṟeṉpatu tuṇintu koḷḷat tōṉṟum, avvāṟu tuṇintukoḷḷum uṇarvuṭiyōr koḷḷiṉ e-ṟu.
eṉavē, aḵtu ellārkkum pulaṉaṉṟu, nalluṇarvuṭi yōrkkē pulaṉeṉpatūum, avar koḷḷac ceyya vēṇṭumeṉpa tūuṅ kūṟiyavāṟu, itaṉāṉē ceyyuḷuṭ payiṉṟu varumeṉpatu kūṟiṉāṉām. avaṟṟiṟku utāraṇam mēṟkāṭṭutum. maṟṟitaṉi uvamameṉṟa teṉṉi? uvamamum uvamikkappaṭum poruḷumāka niṟīik kūṟāṉāyiṉā ṉeṉiṉ, aṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟāṉāyiṉum uvamam pōṉṟu poruḷ koḷḷappaṭutaliṉ ataṉi uvamameṉṟāṉ. aḵtu oppiṉākiya peyareṉpatu; eṉṉi? ivaṟṟi uvamappōliyeṉṟum kūṟumākaliṉ.	(23)
uḷḷuṟi uvama vaki
299.	uvamap pōli ainteṉa moḻipa.
itu, mēṟkūṟiya uḷḷuṟiyuvamam aivakippaṭumeṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) uḷḷuṟiyuvamam aintuvakiyeṉak kūṟuvar pulavar e-ṟu.
aviyintumāmāṟu muṉṉarc(300) collum. itaṉatu payam ēṉiyuvamattukku nilikkaḷam aintu ōtiṉāṉ, avvāṟē itaṟkum nilik kaḷam ōtātu (305) avi pōṟaliṉ, aviyē nilikkaḷamā meṉṟalum ēṉiyuvamattuḷ oru cātiyōṭu oru cātiyiṉiuvamittal vaḻakkaṉ ṟāyiṉum uḷḷuṟi yuvamattiṟku amiyumeṉṟalumeṉpatu.(24)
mēṟkūṟiya uḷḷuṟi aintāvaṉa
300.	tavalaruñ ciṟappiṉat taṉmi nāṭiṉ
	viṉiyiṉum payattiṉum uṟuppiṉum uruviṉum
	piṟappiṉum varūum tiṟattiya leṉpa.
itu, mēṟkūṟiya aintum iviyeṉkiṉṟatu.
(i-ḷ.) viṉi payaṉ mey uruveṉṟa nāṉkiṉāṉum piṟappiṉā ṉum varum mēṟkūṟiya aintum e-ṟu.
uṟuppeṉṟatu meyyiṉi; uṭampiṉi uṟuppeṉpavākalāṉum meyyuvamamellām uṟuppiṉiyēpaṟṟi varutal perumpāṉmiyavāka lāṉum avvāṟu kūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu. 'tavalaruñ ciṟappiṉat taṉmi nāṭiṉ' eṉṟataṉāṉ ēṉi yuvamattiṉum uḷḷuṟi yuvamamē ceyyuṭ kum poruḷilakkaṇattiṟkuñ ciṟantateṉpatu. avi varumāṟu.
"karumpunaṭu pāttik kalitta tāmari
curumpupaci kaḷiyum perumpuṉa lūra
putalva ṉīṉṟavem muyaṅka
latuvē teyyaniṉ mārpucitip patuvē"	(aiṅkuṟu. 65)
eṉpatu viṉiyumavappōli. eṉṉi? tāmariyiṉi viḷippataṟ kaṉṟik karumpu naṭutaṟkuc ceyta pāttiyuḷ, tāṉē viḷinta tāmari curum piṉ paci tīrkku mūraṉeṉṟāḷ. itaṉ karuttu, kātaṟparattiyarkkum iṟparattiyarkkum eṉṟamikkappaṭṭa kōyiluḷ yāmumuḷamāki illaṟam pūṇṭu viruntōmpukiṉṟaṉam atupōla veṉpatākalāṉ uvamattiṟkup piṟitoru poruḷ etirntu uvamañ ceyyātu āṇṭup piṟantaṉavaṟṟoṭu nōkkik karuttiṉāṟ koḷḷavittaliṉ iḵtu uḷḷuṟi yuvama māyiṟṟu. avaṟṟuḷḷum itu curumpu pacikaḷiyun toḻiloṭu viruntōmputaṟṟoḻil uvamaṅkoḷḷa niṉṟamiyiṉ viṉiyuvamappōli yāyiṟṟu. iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟavē itaṉi ip poruṇmit teṉpatellām uṇarumāṟeṉṉi yeṉiṉ, muṉṉart,
"tuṇivoṭu varūun tuṇiviṉōr koḷiṉē" 	(tol. poruḷ. 298)
eṉal vēṇṭiyatu itaṉ arumi nōkkiyaṉṟē yeṉpatu. allākkāṟ 'karumpunaṭu pāttik kalitta tāmari, curumpupaci kaḷiyum perumpuṉa lūra' eṉpatu payamila kūṟalā meṉpatu.
"karicēr vēḻaṅ karumpiṟ pūkkun
tuṟikē ḻūraṉ koṭumi nāṇi
nalla ṉeṉṟum yāmē
yalla ṉeṉṉumem taṭameṉ ṟōḷē1" 	
eṉpatu, payavuvamappōli. itaṉuḷ talimakaṉ koṭumi kūṟiyatallatu akkoṭumik kētuvākiyatoṉṟu viḷaṅkak kūṟiyatilaḷāyiṉum, iḻinta vēḻam uyarnta karumpiṟ pūkkumeṉavē, avaṟku iḻipuyarvām eṉpa toṉṟilli; ellārum iṉpaṅ kōṭaṟkuriyar talimakaṟ keṉṟa miyiṉ yāmum parattiyarum avaṟku ottaṉa meṉṟamiyiṉ avi kūṟiṉāḷeṉpatu.
"nīruṟi kōḻi nīlac cēval
kūrukirp pēṭi vayāa mūra
puḷiṅkāy vēṭkit taṉṟuniṉ
malarnta mārpivaḷ vayāa nōykkē"	(aiṅkuṟu. 51)
eṉpatum atu. nīruṟi kōḻi nīlac cēvali ataṉ kūrukirppeṭi niṉintatu kaṭuñcūlāṉvanta vayāt tīrtaṟ payattatākum, atupōla niṉmārpu niṉintu taṉ vayavunōy tīrum ivaḷumeṉṟavāṟu. puḷiṅkāy vēṭkit teṉpatu, niṉ mārputāṉ ivaḷi nayavātāyiṉum ivaḷ tāṉē niṉ mārpi nayantu payampeṟṟāḷ pōlac cuvikoṇṭu ciṟitu vēṭki taṇitaṟ payattaḷākum, puḷiyaṅkāy niṉiya vāynīr ūṟumāṟu pōla eṉpatu.
"oṉṟē ṉallē ṉoṉṟuveṉ kuṉṟattup
porukaḷiṟu mititta neritāḷ vēṅki
kuṟavar makaḷir kūntaṟ peymmār
niṉṟukoya malaru nāṭaṉoṭu
oṉṟēṉ tōḻi yoṉṟi ṉāṉē"	(kuṟun. 208)
eṉpatu meyyuvamappōli; eṉṉi? mitiyuṇṭu vīḻnta vēṅki kuṟiyuyiroṭu malarntāṟpōla yāṉum uḷēṉāyiṉē ṉeṉṟamiyiṉ.
" 2vaṇṇa voṇṭaḻi nuṭaṅka vāliḻi
yoṇṇuta larivi paṇṇi pāynteṉak
kaṇṇaṟuṅ kuvaḷi nāṟit
taṇṇeṉ ṟiciṉē peruntuṟip puṉalē"	(aiṅkuṟu. 73)
iḵtu, uruvuvamappōli. nī puṉalāṭiya ñāṉṟu paratti pāyn tāṭiya puṉalellān taṇṇeṉṟateṉak kūṟiyavaḻi, attaṭampōla ivaḷ uṟakkalaṅkit teḷintu taṇṇeṉṟāḷeṉpatu karuti yuṇarappaṭṭatu. avaḷoṭu puṉal pāyntāṭiya iṉpac ciṟappuk kēṭṭu niliyāṟṟā ḷeṉpatu karuttu. itu niṟamaṉṟāleṉiṉ, niṟamum paṇpākaliṉ anniṟattōṭu niṟamallāta paṇpuṅ koḷḷappaṭumeṉpatu 'vakipeṟa vanta uvamat tōṟṟam' (tol. poruḷ. 276) eṉpuḻik kūṟiṉāmeṉpatu.
"poykip paḷḷip pulavunāṟu nīrnāy
vāḷi nāḷiri peṟūu mūra
ennalan toliva tāyiṉun
tuṉṉalam perumapiṟart tōynta mārpē"	(aiṅkuṟu. 63)
eṉpatu, piṟappuvamappōli. nalla kulattiṟ piṟantum iḻintārit tōyn tamiyāṉ avar nāṟṟamē nāṟiyatu, avariyē pātukāvāy, mēṟkulattup piṟanta emmit tīṇṭaleṉpāḷ aḵtellām viḷaṅkak kūṟātu 'poykip paḷḷip piṟanta nīrnāy muṉṉāḷ tiṉṟa vāḷimīṉ pulavu nāṟṟattoṭu piṉṉāḷum ataṉiyē vēṇṭum ūraṉ' eṉṟamiyiṉ, parattiyar piṟappu iḻintamiyun talivi piṟappu uyarntamiyuṅ kūṟi avaṉ piṟappiṉ uyarvuṅ kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, itu piṟappuvamappōli yāyiṟṟu.
iviyellāṅ karutik kūṟiṟ ceyyuṭkuc ciṟappāmeṉavum, vāḷātu nīr nāy vāḷi peṟūu mūraṉeṉṟataṉāṉ oru payamiṉṟeṉavuṅ koḷka. 'piṟappoṭu varūun tiṟatta' veṉṟatu. talimakaṉāṉ nikaḻum ivvāṟu tiṟappāṭu vēṟumuḷa veṉpatūuṅ koḷka. avi,
"taṉpārpput tiṉṉu maṉpiṉ mutaliyoṭu
veṇpūm poykit tavaṉū reṉpa, ataṉāṉ
taṉco luṇarntōr mēṉi
poṉpōṟ ceyyu mūrkiḻa vōṉē"	(aiṅkuṟu. 41)
eṉṟavaḻit, taṉ pārpput tiṉṉum aṉpil mutali yeṉpatu tali makaṉatu koṭumikku uvamamāyiṟṟu. veṇpūm poykittu avaṉū reṉpatu talimakaḷ pacappuniṟam paṟṟi uvamamāyiṟṟu. ātalāṉ viṉi yuvamamum uruvuvamamum oru ceyyuḷuḷḷē toṭarntu oruṅku varutalum uṭiyaveṉpatu,
iṉṉum 'tiṟattiyal' eṉṟataṉāṉē, ittaṉmittākiya ūraṉiyā ḷeṉac collutalun, talivaṉūriṉkaṇuḷḷaṉa collat talivaṟkē yaṉṟit talivik kēṟṟa uvamam tōṉṟac ceytalum ēṉiyuvamaṅ kūṟiyavaḻi uḷḷuṟiyuvamaṅ kōṭalum, piṟavāṟu varuvaṉavuḷavāyiṉum ellāṅ koḷka.
"tērvaṇ kōmāṉ ṟēṉū raṉṉavivaḷ"	(aiṅkuṟu. 55)
eṉpatu avaṉūraṉiyāḷeṉa vantatu.
"veṇpūm poykittu avaṉūr" 	(aiṅkuṟu. 41)
eṉat talivaṉūriṉ uḷḷatoṉṟaṉāṉ talivik kuvamam piṟappittavā ṟāyiṟṟu. allākkāl,
"veḷḷi yāmpa laṭikari"	(aiṅkuṟu. 41)
eṉṟataṉāṟ payamiṉṟeṉpatu.	(25)
kiḻaviyum tōḻiyum uḷḷuṟi colla vēṇṭu muṟi
301.	kiḻavi colliṉ avaḷaṟi kiḷavi
	tōḻikku āyiṉ nilampeyarntu uriyātu.
itu, mēṟkūṟiya uḷḷuṟiyuvamattiṟ kāvatōr ilakkaṇam.
(i-ḷ.) aintu vakippaṭṭa uvamappōliyum piṟitoṭu paṭātu piṟappu nōkki uṇarak kūṟiyavaḻi, akkūṟṟut talimakaṭkun tōḻikkum urittāṅkāl, talivikkāyiṉ avaḷaṟiyuṅ karupporuḷāṉē ceppal vēṇṭum; tōḻikkāyiṉ annilattuḷḷaṉa vellāñ collavum peṟum; piṟanilattuḷḷaṉa aṟintu colliṉaḷākac ceyyuḷ ceyyap peṟāḷ e-ṟu.
itaṉatu payam, talimakaḷ innilattuḷḷaṉavellām aṟiyun tuṇip payiṟciyila ḷeṉavum avaḷāyattārāyiṉ innilattuḷḷaṉa aṟiyac citinta tiṉṟeṉavum kūṟiyavāṟu.
"oṉṟē ṉallē ṉoṉṟuveṉ"	(kuṟun. 208)
eṉṉum pāṭṭiṉuṭ, porukaḷiṟu mititta neritāḷ vēṅki maram paṭappiyiluḷḷa tākalāṉun taṉṉāṟ pūkkoyyappaṭu mātalāṉum aḵtu avaḷaṟikiḷavi yeṉappaṭṭatu.
"taṉpārpput tiṉṉu maṉpiṉ mutali"	(aiṅkuṟu. 41)
eṉpatu tōḻi kūṟṟu. eṉṉi? avaṟṟiṉ ceykiyellām aṟi yāḷaṉṟē talimakaḷ, perumpēti yātaliṉeṉpatu. atikārattāṉiṉṟa uḷḷuṟiyuvama meṉpataṉi avaḷaṟi kiḷavi yeṉṟataṟkup peyarp payaṉiliyākavum nilampeyarn turiyāteṉṉum muṟṟuviṉikkup peyarākavum vēṟu vēṟu collikkoḷka.	(26)
talivaṉ uḷḷuṟi kiḷakkumāṟum
oḻintōrkku iṭam varivuiṉṟu ātalum
302.	kiḻavōṟku āyiṉ uraṉoṭu kiḷakkum
	ēṉōrkku ellām iṭamvarivu iṉṟē.
ituvum atu.
(i-ḷ.) kiḻavōṉ collum uḷḷuṟiyuvaman taṉuraṉuṭimi tōṉṟac collappaṭum; ēṉōreṉappaṭṭa pāṅkaṉum pāṇaṉu mutalāyiṉōr colluṅkāli iṭam variyappaṭātu, mēṟkūṟiya vakiyāṉē, tāntām aṟinta kiḷaviyāṉum nilampeyarnturiyāta kiḷaviyāṉum en nilattuḷḷa poruḷāṉum uḷḷuṟiyuvamam collap peṟupa. e-ṟu.
"karuṅkōṭ ṭerumic ceṅkaṭ puṉiṟṟāk
kātaṟ kuḻavikku ūṟumuli maṭukkum
nunti nummūr varutum
oṇṭoṭi maṭanti niṉṉiyām peṟiṉē"	(aiṅkuṟu. 92)
eṉṟavaḻit, tāy pōṉṟu nummit taliyaḷippaleṉat talimakaṉ talimi tōṉṟa uraṉoṭu kiḷantavāṟu kāṇka. oḻintōrkkāyiṉ variyaṟiyiṉmiyiṟ kāṭṭāmāyiṉām. avi vantuḻik (kali. 69) kāṇka.	(27)
iṉpamum tuṉpamum tōṉṟa uḷḷuṟiyuvamam collappaṭutal
303.	iṉituṟu kiḷaviyun tuṉiyuṟu kiḷaviyum
	uvama maruṅkiṉ tōṉṟu meṉpa.
ituvum mēlataṟkē yāvatoru vēṟupāṭuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) mēṟ kūṟappaṭṭa uḷḷuṟiyuvamam iṉpa tuṉpaṅkaḷ tōṉṟac collavumpaṭum e-ṟu.
"kaḻaṉi māattu viḷintuku tīmpaḻam
paḻaṉa vāḷi katūum ūraṉ"	(kuṟun. 8)
eṉpatu, palavaki yiṉpamum varuntātu peṟuvareṉpataṟku uvamamāki varutaliṉ iṉituṟu kiḷavi yeṉappaṭṭatu.
"tāycāp piṟakkum puḷḷik kaḷvaṉoṭu
piḷḷi tiṉṉu mutalittu"	(aiṅkuṟu. 24)
eṉpatu, talimakaṉ koṭumi kūṟiṉamiyiṉ tuṉiyuṟu kiḷaviyāyiṟṟu.
ivviru pakutiyum paṭac ceyyappaṭum mēṟkūṟiya uḷḷuṟi yuvamameṉpatu itaṉ karuttu.
'maruṅku' eṉṉum mikiyāṉē, ēṉiyuvamattiṉkaṇṇum ippakuti koḷḷappaṭum. avi,
"marunteṉiṉ maruntē vippeṉiṉ vippē"	(kuṟun. 71)
eṉpatu, iṉituṟu kiḷavi.
"kārattiṉ veyyen tōḷ"	(ain. aim. 24)
eṉpatu, tuṉiyuṟu kiḷavi.	        (28)
talivi uḷḷuṟi kūṟum nilam
304.	kiḻavōṭku uvamam īriṭattu urittē.
iḵtu, avvuḷḷuṟiyuvamatti variyaṟuttuṇarttal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) talimakaḷ iraṇṭiṭattallatu uḷḷuṟiyuvamam collap peṟāḷ e-ṟu.
iraṇṭiṭameṉpaṉa, marutamum neytalum. annilattup piṟanta poruḷ paṟṟiyallatu uḷḷuṟiyuvamañ collutal kiḻattikkurit taṉṟeṉpatu karuttu. ivviṭattu urimiyuṭit teṉavē kuṟiñcikkaṇ attuṇiyurittaṉ ṟeṉṟavāṟu.
"tāmari vaṇṭūtu paṉimala rāru mūra
yāri yōniṟ pulakkēm"	(akam. 46)
eṉṟavaḻi vaṇṭūtu paṉimalareṉap piṟarkkumuriya makaḷireṉavum avari nayappāyeṉavum uḷḷuṟiyuvamam marutattukkaṇ vantatu.
"aṉṉi vāḻivēṇ ṭaṉṉi kaḻiya
muṇṭakam malarun taṇkaṭaṟ cērppaṉ
eṉtōḷ tuṟantaṉa ṉāyiṉ
eṉṉāṅ kollava ṉayanta tōḷē.''	(aiṅkuṟu. 108)
eṉpatu neytal. itaṉuṭ 'kaḻiya muṇṭakam malarum' eṉa muḷḷuṭiya taṉip pūmalarumeṉṟu uḷḷuṟuttataṉāṉ, iruvar kāmattuṟik kaṇṇum orutali iṉṉā orutali iṉiteṉṟāḷeṉpatu. 'eṉtōḷ tuṟantaṉaṉ' eṉpatu, muḷḷuṭimiyōṭokka, 'eṉṉāṅkollava ṉayantōḷ tōḷ' eṉṟavaḻi avaḷ aṉpiṟṟiriyāmi kūṟiṉamiyiṉ muṇṭaka malarcciyō ṭoppikkappaṭum. piṟavum aṉṉa,
"kuṉṟak kuṟavaṉ pulvēy kurampi
maṉṟā ṭiḷamaḻi maṟikkum nāṭaṉ
puriyōṉ vāḻi tōḻi viripeya
larumpaṉi yaḷiiya kūtirp
peruntaṇ vāṭiyiṉ muntuvan taṉaṉē''	(aiṅkuṟu. 252)
eṉṉuṅ kuṟiñcippāṭṭiṉuḷ, vaṟumi kūrnta pulvēy kurampiyi maḻi puṟamaṟittāṟ pōla vāṭi ceyyum nōytīrkka vantā ṉeṉṟu uḷḷuṟiyuvamañ ceytavāṟu kaṇṭukoḷka.
iṉik, 'kiḻavōṭ kuvamam piriviṭat turittu' eṉpatu pāṭamāka urippārumuḷar. yātāṉumoru nilattāyiṉum pirintiruntaviṭattu uḷḷuṟiyuvamaṅ kūṟappeṟuṅ kiḻattiyeṉpatu itaṉ karuttu. perun taṇvāṭiyiṉ muntuvantōṉeṉpatu pirivaṉṟākaliṉ īriṭameṉṟalē valiteṉpatu.	(29)
talivaṉ uḷḷuṟi kūṟutaṟku variyaṟi illi eṉal
305.	kiḻavōṟ kāyiṉ iṭamvari viṉṟē.
mēl 'uraṉoṭu kiḷakkum' (tol poruḷ-302) eṉṟatallatu iṉṉavaḻic collappeṟun talimakaṉeṉṟilaṉ, ataṉāṉ avaṟku ellā nilaṉum uriyavāmeṉkiṉṟāṉeṉpatu.
(i-ḷ.) talimakaṟku iṭavariyaṟi illi e-ṟu.
variyaṟiyillaṉavaṟṟukku variyaṟi kūṟāmē muṭiyāvō veṉiṉ, aṅṅaṉamāyiṉuṅ kiḻattikkun tōḻikkum iṭam (301) variyaṟuttataṉik kaṇṭa māṇākkaṉ ivvāṟē talimakaṟkum iṭam variyaṟi yuṇṭukoleṉṟu karutiṟ karutaṟkaveṉṟaṟku itu kūṟiṉā ṉeṉpatu.	(30)
tōḻiyum ceviliyum uḷḷuṟi kūṟumāṟu
306.	tōḻiyuñ ceviliyum poruntuvaḻi nōkkik
	kūṟutaṟ kuriyar koḷvaḻi yāṉa.
itu, tōḻiyuñ ceviliyum uḷḷuṟiyuvamaṅ kūṟumiṭamuṇarttu tal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) kālattiṟkum iṭattiṟkum poruntumāṟṟāṉ uḷḷuṟi yuvamaṅ kūṟaṟkuriyar tōḻiyuñ ceviliyum (e - ṟu).
kālamumiṭaṉum poruntutaleṉpatu veḷippaṭak kiḷavātu muṉṉattāṉ maṟittuc collavēṇṭiyavaḻi avvāṟu colluvar avaru meṉṟavāṟu, iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟavē, 
'ēṉōrk kellā miṭamvari viṉṟu'	(302)
eṉṟavaḻi, ellārum uḷḷuṟiyuvamañ collaṟkuriyareṉpatu paṭṭatu. ataṉi, naṟṟāyum āyattārun tantiyun taṉṉiyarum uḷḷuṟi yuvamam kūṟāreṉavum, tōḻi kūṟiṉ nilam peyarnturiyāta poruḷāṉ oruvaḻik kūṟumeṉavum, cevilikkāyiṉ iṭamvariviṉ ṟeṉappaṭṭa vakiyāṉ poruntumvaḻik kūṟutaṟkuriyaḷeṉavuṅ kūṟiṉāṉām iccūt tirattāṉeṉpatu. 'koḷvaḻi'yeṉṟataṉāṉ, tōḻikkup pōla nilam peyarnturiyāta poruḷāṉ uḷḷuṟiyuvamaṅ kūṟutalē cevilikku muritteṉpatu koḷka. maṟṟiviyellām akapporuṭkē yuriyavāka vitantōtiya teṉṉi puṟapporuṭku vārātaṉapōla eṉiṉ, āṇṭu varutal aritākaliṉ ivvāṟu akattiṟkē kūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu.
"vaṉpulak kēḷirkku varuvirun tayarum
meṉpula vippi ṉaṉṉāṭṭup poruna"	(puṟam. 42)
eṉṟakkāṟ, pakivēntari veṉṟikoḷḷuṅkāl avar tāmē tattam poruḷ piṟarkkaḷippāreṉṉum poruḷ tōṉṟiṉum tōṉṟu meṉpatal latu oru taliyāka uḷḷuṟiyuvamaṅ kōṭal vēṇṭuvataṉṟu; eṉṉi?
"tāycāp piṟakkum puḷḷik kaḷvaṉoṭu
piḷḷi tiṉṉu mutalit tavaṉūr"	(aiṅkuṟu. 24)
eṉṟāṟpōlak kūṟātu, annāṭṭuk karuṅkaḷamar mutalāyiṉār varuntā maṟ peṟum poruḷ piṟanāṭṭārkku viruntuceyyat takumeṉṟu annāṭṭiṉatu vaḷami kūṟiṉamiyiṉeṉpatu. tōḻi poruntiyavaḻik kūṟumāṟu muṉ kāṭṭappaṭṭaṉa. cevili poruntuvaḻik kūṟuvaṉavuṅ kaṇṭukoḷka. ilakkaṇamuṇmiyiṉ aviyum uḷaveṉpatu karuttu. 	(31)
vēṟupaṭa varum uvamaṅkaḷum koḷḷappaṭutal
307.	vēṟupaṭa vanta vuvamat tōṟṟam
	kūṟiya maruṅkiṉ koḷvaḻik koḷāal.
itu, mēlellām iruvakiyuvamamuṅ kūṟi iṉṉum ēṉi yuvamap pakutiyē kūṟuvāṉ eytātateytuvittatu.
(i-ḷ.) vēṟupaṭavanta uvamattōṟṟam- vēṟupāṭu tōṉṟa vanta uvamaccāti; tōṟṟameṉiṉum piṟappeṉiṉuñ cātiyeṉiṉum okkum; kūṟiya maruṅkiṟ koḷvaḻik koḷāal- aṅṅaṉam vēṟupaṭa vantaṉavā yiṉum mēṟkūṟiya pakutiyāṉē koḷḷumiṭaṉaṟintu koḷuttuka e-ṟu.
koḷuvutaleṉpatu 'koḷāal' eṉpatāyiṟṟu.
vēṟupaṭavarutaleṉpatu; uvamattukkum poruṭkum oppumi māṟu paṭak kūṟutalum, oppumi kūṟātu peyar pōlvaṉavaṟṟu māttiriyāṉē  maṟuttuk kūṟutalum, oppumi maṟuttup poruḷi nāṭṭik kūṟutalum, oppumi maṟuttavaḻip piṟitōruvamam nāṭṭutalum, 4uvamamum poruḷum muṟkūṟi niṟīip piṉṉar maṟṟiya ovvāveṉṟa lum, uvamattukku irukuṇaṅkoṭuttup poruḷiṉi vāḷātu kūṟuṅkāl uvamatti iraṇṭākki oṉṟaṟkuk kūṟiya aṭi oṉṟaṟkuk kūṟātukūṟutalum, oppumi kuṟivupaṭa uvamittu maṟṟoru kuṇaṅ koṭuttu nirapputalum, ovvāk karuttiṉāṉ oppumi kōṭalum, uvamattiṟkaṉṟi uvamattiṟku ētuvākiya poruṭkuc cila aṭikūṟi av aṭiyāṉē uvamikkappaṭum poruḷiṉic ciṟappittalum, uvamāṉat tiṉi uvamēyamākkiyum atu vilakkiyuṅ kūṟutalum, iraṇṭu poru ḷāṉē vevvēṟu kūṟiyavaḻi oṉṟu oṉṟaṟku uvamameṉpatu koḷḷa vittalum, iṉṉōraṉṉa vellām 'vēṟupaṭavanta uvamattōṟṟam' eṉappaṭum.
ivaṟṟik 'kūṟiya maruṅkiṟ koḷuttutal' eṉpatu, muṟkūṟiya ēṉiyuvamattiṉpālum piṟkūṟiya uḷḷuṟiyuvamattiṉpālum paṭuttu uṇarappaṭumeṉpatu. ēṉiyuvamattiṉpāṟpaṭuttaleṉpatu, viṉi payaṉ mey uru eṉṟa nāṉkumpaṟṟi (276) varutalum avaṟṟukku ōtiya aivaki nilikkaḷaṉumpaṟṟi (279-280) varutalumeṉak koḷka. uḷḷuṟiyuvamat tiṉpāṟpaṭuttaleṉpatu, ivvēṉiyuvamampōla uvamiyum poruḷu māki vēṟuvēṟu viḷaṅka vārātu kuṟippiṉāṟ koḷḷavarutaliṉ. ikkaruttiṉāṉē itaṉi īṇṭu vippāṉāyiṟṟu. varalāṟu:
"viyaṅ kāvalar vaḻimoḻin toḻukap
pōkam vēṇṭip potuccoṟ poṟāatu
iṭañciṟi teṉṉum ūkkan turappa
voṭuṅkā vuḷḷat tōmpā vīkik
kaṭantaṭu tāṉic cēra lātaṉi
yāṅkaṉa mottiyō vīṅkucelaṉ maṇṭilam
poḻuteṉa variti puṟaṅkoṭut tiṟatti
māṟi varuti malimaṟin toḷitti
akaliru vicumpi ṉāṉum
pakalviḷaṅ kaliyāṟ palkatir virittē''	(puṟam. 8)
eṉṉum pāṭṭiṉuḷ, "kaṭantaṭu tāṉic cēralātaṉi" eṉṉun tuṇi uvamattiṟku vanta aṭiyiṉip poruṭku maṟuttuk koḷḷa vittāṉeṉpatu. iṉip "poḻuteṉa variti" eṉpatu toṭaṅkip pāṭṭu muṭikāṟum poruṭkuriya aṭiyiṉi uvamattiṟku maṟuttuk koḷḷa vittāṉeṉpatu. eṉṉi? veñcuṭarvaḻit tōṉṟiya aracaṉit taṇcuṭarōṭu uvamippāṉ 'poruḷē uvamañ' ceytamiyā ṉeṉpatu.
'poruḷē uvamañ ceytaṉar moḻiyiṉu' (284) meṉṟataṉāṟ poruḷiṉi uvamamākak kūṟātu uḷḷuṟiyuvamam pōlak koḷḷa vittup piṉṉar uvamattiṟku aṭiyāyavaṟṟuḷ,
"viyaṅ kāvalar vaḻimoḻin toḻuka"	
eṉṟāṉ.
vaḻimoḻitaleṉpatu, vēṟṟaracarkkut tamtaṉmiyeṉa vēṟiṉṟit taṉṉakappaṭuttal; ākalāṉ, tattam oḷiyoṭu paṭṭu oḻinta kōḷuñ cellat tāṉuñ cellum matiyameṉṟu etirmaṟuttuk kuṟṟaṅkūṟum kuṟippup paṭa vittāṉeṉpatu.
"pōkam vēṇṭip potuccoṟ poṟāatu"	
eṉavē, iṉpanukarvumuṟṟuc ciṟappillāk kaṭṭuri yeytāṉeṉavum avaṉōṭu uvamikkiṉṟa matiyamāyiṉ irupatteḻuvar makaḷiroṭu pōkan tuyttuc ciṟappillāta kaṭṭuri puṉiyumeṉṟum etirmaṟuttuk koḷḷa vittāṉ. ciṟappiṉmi yeṉpatu, ellārkkum ottavāṟṟāṉ aṟañ ceyyātu urōkaṇimēṟ kaḻiperuṅ kātalaṉeṉappaṭutal pōlvaṉa.
"iṭañciṟi teṉṉu mūkkan turappa"	
eṉavē, eññāṉṟun taṉṉellikkaṇṇē varum mati maṇṭila meṉṟu etirmaṟuttuk koḷḷappaṭum. "oṭuṅkāvuḷḷam" eṉavē, ammatiyam tēyn toṭuṅkumeṉpatu koḷḷappaṭum. "ōmpā vīki" eṉavē, nāṭōṟum orōvoru kaliyākap palluyirkkum iṉpam payakkumāṟṟāṉ taruva tallātu tāṉuṭiyatellām orukālē koṭāta matiyameṉappaṭum."kaṭan taṭu tāṉi" eṉavē, matikkut tāṉiyākiya tārakiyellām pakikkatirākiya paruti maṇṭilattukkut tōṟku meṉṟāṉām. ivvāṟṟāṉ uvamāṉa aṭiyellām etir maṟuttuk koḷḷappaṭṭaṉa.
iṉip, poruṭkuriya aṭiyum avvāṟē etirmaṟuttuk koḷḷappaṭumeṉṟavāṟu. "vīṅkucelaṉ maṇṭila" meṉavē, kaṭiyā yiṉār katiyiṟ cellu matiyameṉṟu pāṭṭuṭit talivaṉ taliyāyi ṉār katiyiṟ cellu meṉṟāṉ. "poḻuteṉa variti" yeṉavē, nāṭōṟum nāḻiki vēṟupaṭṭu eṟitti eṉṟataṉāṉ avaṉ poḻutu ceyyāṉeṉavum, "puṟak koṭut tiṟatti" yeṉavē, tōṟṟōr pōṉṟu oḷi maḻuṅkic celkiṉṟāy eṉpataṉāṉ cuṭar pōla viḷaṅkip piṟar tōṟṟōṭak kāyum ivaṉeṉavum, "māṟi varuti" yeṉavē, tiṅkaṭōṟum māṟip piṟatti yeṉpataṉāṉ ivaṉ nilipeṟṟā ṉeṉavum, "mali maṟin toḷitti" yeṉavē, malicārntavaḻit tōṉṟāyeṉpa taṉāṉ ivaṉ taṉṉāṭṭu mali mīkkūṟumeṉavum. "akaliru vicumpiṉāṉu" meṉavē, ivaṉ ivvulakattum nilipēṟuṭiyaṉeṉavum, "pakal viḷaṅkāy" eṉavē, ivaṉ iru poḻutum viḷaṅkumeṉavuṅ koḷḷap paṭum. muṟpakutiyum piṟpakutiyum vēṟupaṭutaliṉ vēṟupaṭa vantatāyiṟṟu. maṟṟiyavum aṉṉa.
ikkūṟiya aṭi yellām, viṉi payaṉ meyuruveṉak 'kūṟiya maruṅkiṟ koḷvaḻik koḷumuvavamākak' kāṭṭuvaṉavaṟṟiṟkum iḵtok kum.
"kaṇṇa ṉavaṉivaṉ māṟaṉ kamaḻtuḻāyk
kaṇṇi yavaṟkivaṟku vēmputār - vaṇṇamum
māya ṉavaṉivaṉ cēyaṉ marapoṉṟē
āya ṉavaṉivaṉ kō"	
eṉpatu, peyarun tāru mutalāyiṉa paṟṟi māyaṉōṭu uvamaṅkaruti maṟut turittavāṟu. peyareṉpatu poruḷuṇarttutaliṉ ataṉi vaṭiviṉ pāṟ paṭuttuṇarka.
"aṭinōkki ṉāḻkaṭal vaṇṇaṉṟaṉ mēṉip
paṭinōkkiṟ piṅkoṉṟit tārōṉ- muṭinōkkit
tērvaḷava ṉāta ṟeḷintēṉṟaṉ ceṉṉimēl
āralaṅka ṟōṉṟiṟṟuk kaṇṭu"	
eṉpataṉuḷ, āḻkaṭal vaṇṇaṉiyuṅ koṉṟittārōṉiyum uvamam kūṟiyavaṟṟi maṟuttut tērvaḷavaṉeṉat teḷintēṉeṉap poruḷiyē nāṭṭutaliṉ aḵtu, uvamam vēṟupaṭa vantatāyiṟṟu.
"intira ṉeṉṉi ṉiraṇṭēkaṇ ṇēṟūrnta
vantarattā ṉeṉṉiṟ piṟiyilli - yantarattuk
kōḻiyā ṉeṉṉiṉ mukamoṉṟē kōtiyi
āḻiyā ṉeṉṟuṇaraṟ pāṟṟu"	
eṉpataṉuḷ, intiraṉiyum iṟiyōṉiyum murukaṉiyum oppu maṟuttu, neṭiyōṉi uvamaṅkūṟaliṉ, oppumi maṟuttup piṟitu nāṭṭiyatu.
"cuṟṟuviṟ kāmaṉuñ cōḻar perumakaṉāṅ
koṟṟappōrk kiḷḷiyuṅ kēḻovvār - poṟṟoṭīi
yāḻi yuṭiyāṉ makaṉmāyaṉ; cēyaṉē
kōḻi yuṭiyāṉ makaṉ."	
eṉpataṉāṉ, uvamamum poruḷum muṉ oruṅkuniṟīip piṉṉar ovvāmi kūṟutaliṉ ituvum piṉṉum vēṟupaṭa vantatāyiṟṟu.
"puṉaṉāṭar kōmāṉum pūntuḻāy mālum
viṉivakiyāṉ vēṟu paṭupa - puṉaṉāṭa
ṉēṟṟeṟintu māṟṟalarpā leytiyapār māyavaṉ
ēṟṟirantu koṇṭamiyā ṉiṉṟu"	
eṉpatum atu.
"ūrkkuṟu mākkaḷ veṇkōṭu kaḻāaliṉ
nīrttuṟi paṭiyum peruṅkaḷiṟu pōla
iṉiyi peruma emakkē"	(puṟam. 94)
eṉṉum pāṭṭiṉuḷ uvamamākiya poruḷiṉi yāṉiyuṅ kaṭāmumeṉa iraṇṭākki yāṉikkē ūrkkuṟumākkaḷ veṇkōṭukaḻāliṉ nīrt tuṟipaṭiyumeṉṉum aṭikūṟi ūrkkuṟumākkaḷ pōlvārit tuṉṉaruṅ kaṭāattiṟkuc collāmiyiṉ ituvum vēṟupaṭa vanta uvamattōṟṟa meṉappaṭṭatu.
"mutirkōṅkiṉ mukiyeṉa mukañceyta kurumpiyeṉap
peyaṟuḷi mukiḻeṉap peruttaniṉ ṉiḷamuli"	(kali. 56)
eṉṟavaḻi, mutirkōṅkiṉ mukiyum muṟṟiya kurumpiyum periyavāka liṉ avipōlap perutta niṉ iḷamuliyeṉṟal ottatu, peyaṟuḷi mukuḷañ ciṟitāka avaṟṟōṭu ataṉi uṭaṉkūṟi ippeyaṟuḷi mukuḷat tiṟkillāta perumikkuṇam poruṭkup piṉṉar vitantu kūṟutaliṉ atuvum vēṟupaṭavanta uvamiyāyiṟṟu.
"makkaḷē pōlvar kayava ravaraṉṉa
voppāri yāṅkaṇṭa til''	(kuṟaḷ. 1071)
eṉpatu ovvāpporuḷi oppumikoṇṭatu. eṉṉi? makkaḷik kayavar ovvāreṉṉuṅ karuttiṉāṉ makkaḷ pōlvar kayavareṉṟami yiṉ atuvum vēṟupaṭavanta uvamamāyiṟṟu.
"neṭuntōṭ ṭirumpaṉi nīrniḻal puriyak 
kuṟumpala murinta kuṉṟucēr ciṟuneṟi"	
eṉpataṉuḷ, uvamamākiya niḻal poruṭkeytiyatu viṉi uvamamaṉṟē? ataṟku neṭuntōṭum perumiyum aṭiyākak kūṟiṉāṉ; kūṟavē, paṉiniḻalōṭokkuṅ kuṉṟañcēr ciṟuneṟi muṭintavaḻic ceṉṟu pukum ūrkkuvamam neṭuntōṭeṉṟu koḷḷa vittamiyiṉ, atuvum vēṟupaṭavanta uvamamāyiṟṟu.
"maṇpaṭutōṭ kiḷḷi matavēḻam māṟṟaracar
veṇkuṭiyit tēytta vekuḷiyāl - viṇpaṭarntu
pāyuṅko leṉṟu paṉimatiyun taṉṉuruvan
tēyun teḷivicumpi ṉiṉṟu"	
eṉpataṉuḷ, uvamāṉattiṉi uvamēyamākki ataṉiyē vilakkiṉāṉ. eṉṉi? veṇkuṭiyeṉṟu yāṉi kuttumeṉṟu matiyiṉik kuṭi yuṭaṉoppippāṉ, matiyiṉik kuṭiyākavē kūṟit, 'tēyunteḷivicum piṉiṉṟu' eṉpataṉāṟ kuṭiyōṭu uvamam kūṟiyati vilakkiṉamiyiṉ atuvum vēṟupaṭavanta uvamattōṟṟamāyiṟṟu.
"akara mutala veḻuttellām āti
pakavaṉ mutaṟṟē yulaku"	
eṉṟavaḻi iraṇṭu poruḷ vēṟuvēṟu kūṟiyataṉṟi, akara mutala eḻuttellām atupōla eṉṟāṉum, ātipakavaṉ mutaṟṟu ulakam atupōla eṉ ṟāṉum oṉṟākat tuṇiyumāṟṟāṉ uvamamum poruḷuṅ kūṟāmiyiṉ, atuvum vēṟupaṭa vanta uvamamāyiṟṟu. piṟavum aṉṉa. ivi ēṉi yuvamattiṟkellām potuvilakkaṇam.	(32)
ovvāteṉak kūṟi uvamamākkutal
308.	orīik kūṟalu marīiya paṇpē.
ituvum, ēṉiyuvamattiṟkāvatōr ilakkaṇamuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) orīikkūṟalum- okkumeṉak kūṟātu ovvāteṉak kūṟuta lum; ummi iṟantatu taḻīiyiṟṟu; marīiya paṇpu-atuvum uvamamātaṟku aṭippaṭavanta vaḻakku e-ṟu.
utāraṇam:
"yāṅkaṉa mottiyō vīṅkucelaṉ maṇṭilam"	(puṟam. 8) 
eṉavum,
"mātar mukampō loḷiviṭa valliyēṟ
kātali vāḻi mati"	(kuṟaḷ. 1118)
eṉavum varum.
"niṉṉō raṉiyinī"	(paripāṭal 1)
eṉpatum atu.
ivaṟṟuḷ, yāṅṅaṉam ottiyōveṉpatu ovvāyeṉṉum poruṭṭu. 'kātali vāḻimati' yeṉṟavaḻiyum yāṉ kātaliyāmiyāṉ matiyamē avaḷ vāṇmuka movvāyeṉṟaliṉ orīik kūṟiṟṟu. ituvum marīiya paṇpākalāṉum uḷḷuṟiyuvamampōlak kuṟippiṉāṉ uvamaṅ kōṭa loppumiyāṉum īṇṭu vittāṉeṉpatu. paṇpeṉṟataṉāṉ atuvum ilakkaṇattōṭokkum eṉṟavāṟu. ivaṟṟiyum vēṟupaṭa vanta uvamameṉṉāmōveṉiṉ, uvamamum avvaḻi māṟupaṭa varumāṟu vēṟupaṭavanta uvamattuk kūṟiṉāṉ; uvamamiṉmi kūṟutalum uvamameṉappaṭu meṉṟaṟku itu kūṟiṉāṉeṉpatu. ivaṟṟuḷḷum vēṟupaṭa vanta ilakkaṇam mēliccūttirattuḷ aṭaṅkum.	(33)
poruṭṭup perumiyum ciṟumiyum tōṉṟāmalum
uvamam kūṟappaṭutal
309.	uvamat taṉmiyum uritteṉa moḻipa
	payaṉili purinta vaḻakkat tāṉa.
iḵtu, eytātatu eytuvittatu; mēl ēṉiyuvamaṅ kūṟuṅkāl uvamikkappaṭum poruṭkup perumiyuñ ciṟumiyuñ ciṟappak kūṟal vēṇṭumeṉṟāṉ, avvāṟaṉṟiyum uvamam koḷḷappaṭumeṉ ṟamiyiṉ.
(i-ḷ.) uvamattaṉmiyum uritteṉa moḻipa- vikāravakiyāṟ perumiyuñ ciṟumiyum oruporuṭkuk kūṟātu paṭṭāṅku uvamaṅ kūṟutalum uritteṉṟu colluvar āciriyar; payaṉili purinta vaḻakkattāṉa- ataṉāṉum oru payaṉ tōṉṟac colluta ṉeṟippāṭ ṭiṉkaṇ e-ṟu.
atu,
"pāri pāri yeṉṟupala vētti
yoruvaṟ pukaḻvar cennāp pulavar"	(puṟam. 107)
eṉṉum pāṭṭiṉuḷ ulakaḷittaṟku māriyum uṇṭeṉa, māriyi uvamittuc ciṟappittukkūṟuvāṉ, māriyic ciṟappittup pāṭṭuṭit talivaṉākiya pāriyi uyarttuk kūṟātāṉ pōla iyalpiṉāṉ uvamam kūṟiṉāṉām; itu mārikkum pārikkum ōriḻivilliyeṉṉun taṉmip paṭak kūṟavē, avaṉuyarvu kūṟutaliṟ payaṉilipurinta vaḻakkeṉap paṭumeṉpatu.
"kolviṉip polinta kūrṅkuṟum puḻukiṉ
villōr tūṇi vīṅkap peyta
appunuṉi ēyppa arumpiya iruppic
ceppaṭa raṉṉa ceṅkuḻi yakantō
ṟiḻuti ṉaṉṉa tīmpuḻal tuyvāy
uḻutukāṇ tuḷiya vāki ārkaḻalpu
āli vāṉiṟ kāloṭu pāṟit
tuppi ṉaṉṉa ceṅkōṭ ṭiyaviṉ
neyttōr mīmici niṇattiṟ parikkum . . . " 	(akam. 9)
eṉpatum,
"nīḷari yilavat talaṅkuciṉi payanta
pūḷiyam pacuṅkāy puṭivirin taṉṉa
varippuṟa vaṇiloṭu karuppi yāṭātu
yāṟṟaṟal puriyum verinuṭik koḻumaṭal
vēṟṟali yaṉṉa vinnuti neṭuntōṭṭu
īttili vēynta veyppuṟak kurampi"	(perumpāṇ. 83-8)
eṉṟāṟ pōlvaṉavum atu.
"kolviṉip polinta"	(akam. 9)
eṉpataṉuḷ, innilattiṉ makkaḷ ampiṉi uvamamākki āṇṭiya vākiya iruppiyiṉ pūṅkotti uvamikkappaṭum poruḷākkiyum, uvama nilattiṟkēṟṟa veṇṇeyttiraḷoṭu kaḻaṉṟa pūviṉi uvamañ ceytum, annilattiyalpu kūṟiṉamiyiṉ, atu payaṉilipurinta vaḻak keṉap paṭṭatu. piṟavum aṉṉa.
'uvamattaṉmiyum' eṉṟa ummiyāṉ uvamattaṉmiyēyaṉṟi vāḷātu taṉmi kūṟutalum annilattiṟkē payaṉiliyeṉappaṭuvaṉavuṅ koḷka; avi,
"māṉṟōṟ paḷḷi makavoṭu muṭaṅki
yīṉpiṇa voḻiyap pōki nōṉkā
ḻirumputali yātta tiruntukaṇi viḻukkō
luḷivāyc curiyiṉ miḷira miṇṭi 
yirunilak karampip paṭunī ṟāṭi
nuṇpul laṭakkiya veṇpa leyiṟṟiyar
pārvi yātta paṟitāḷ viḷavi
ṉīḻaṉ muṉṟi ṉilavuraṟ peytu
kuṟuṅkā ḻulakki yōcci"	(perumpāṇ. 89-97)
eṉṟavaḻi uvamañceyyātu annilattiyalpu kūṟappaṭṭatāyiṉum, uvamat tāṟ poruṭpeṟṟi tōṉṟac ceytāṟpōla annilattiṟkup payappāṭu veḷippaṭac ceyyāmiyiṉ, uvamavilakkaṇattuḷ itaṉi ilēciṉāṟ koṇṭāmeṉpatu; eṉṉiyeṉiṉ,
"marapē tāṉum"	(tol. cey. 80)
eṉpuḻi nāṟcolliyaleṉac coṟṟaṉmiyum (poruḷ taṉmiyum) kūṟappaṭṭamiyiṉ uvamārāycciyuḷ atu (poruḷ taṉmi) kūṟāṉeṉpatu. ummi iṟantatu taḻīiyiṟṟu; eṉṉi? uyarntataṉ mēṟṟaṉṟi uyarpiḻipuṭittallāta taṉmiyuvamamuṅ koḷka veṉṟamiyiṉ.	(34)
taṭumāṟu uvamamum nīkkappaṭāmi
310.	taṭumā ṟuvamam kaṭivari yiṉṟē.
ituvum. uvamattiṟkēyāvatōr ilakkaṇamuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) taṭumāṟuvamam- uvamamum poruḷum vēṟu niṟīi itu pōlum ituveṉṉātu avviraṇṭaṉiyum uvamamuṟac collun taṭu māṟuvamam;  iṉi avvāṟaṉṟi uvamattip poruḷākkiyum poruḷi uvama mākkiyun taṭumāṟac collutalun taṭumāṟuvamameṉappaṭum; kaṭivari iṉṟu- avviraṇṭum uvamameṉṟu collaṟpāṭṭiṟ kaṭiyappaṭā e-ṟu.
"arimalar āyntaka ṇammā kaṭici
tirumukamun tiṅkaḷuñ cettut - terumantu
viyattum vāṉattuñ cellā taṇaṅkāki
yiyattu niṉṟa tarā"	(poykiyār)
eṉpataṉuḷ, uvamiyiṉiyum poruḷiṉiyum vēṟu vēṟu tuṇiyātu aiyuṟṟu viyattum vāṉattuñ cellātu arāveṉṟamiyiṉ itu taṭumāṟuvamamāyiṟṟu.
"taḷipeṟṟu vikiya taṇcuṉi nīlam
aḷipeṟṟār kaṇpōṉ malaru- maḷipeṟṟa
nallār tirumukat tōṟṟat taḷipeṟṟa
kallāram pōṉmalaruṅ kaṇ"	
eṉpuḻi, nīlattoṭu kaṇṇiṉiyum kaṇṇiṉoṭu nīlattiṉiyum oṉṟaṟ koṉṟu uvamamākkiyum poruḷākkiyum oruṅkē taṭumāṟak kūṟiṉami yiṉ ituvun taṭumāṟuvamameṉappaṭṭatu. piṟavum aṉṉa.	(35)
uvamikku uvami illi yātal
311.	aṭukkiya tōṟṟam viṭuttal paṇpē.
iḵtu, eytiyatu maṟuttatu; eṉṉi? vēṟu oru poruḷōṭu oru poruḷi uvamittu niṟīi apporuḷoṭu piṟitoru poruḷi yuvamittalum uvamameṉṟu koḷḷuvāṉāyiṉum atu koḷḷappaṭātu, poruḷ viḷaṅkāmiyiṉeṉak kūṟi vilakkiyamiyiṉ.
(i-ḷ.) aṭukkiya tōṟṟam - uvamamum poruḷum niṟutti aṭukkiya tōṟṟam; viṭuttal paṇpu - ciṟappiṟṟākak koḷḷappaṭātu 
e-ṟu.
"matiyat taṉṉa vāṇmukam pōlum
potiyaviḻ tāmarip putuppūm poyki"	
eṉṟakkāl, matiyattaṉṉa vāṇmukattiṉit tāmari yeṉṟamiyiṉ avi oṉṟu oṉṟaṉoṭu poruntāveṉpatu karuttu.
"ilaṅkuvaḷi yaṉṉa nalaṅkē ḻāmpaṟ
pōti ṉaṉṉa tātaviḻ kiti"	
eṉṟakkāl, oṉṟu oṉṟaṉōṭu oruvaṇṇattatāy uvamattiṟkēṟpiṉum oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu uvamamāy niṉṟatu. niṉṟuṟa maṟṟu ataṉōṭu uvamaṅ koḷḷappaṭātu. itu variyaṟiyuṭimiyiṉ vilakkappaṭṭatu. maṟṟu,
"īrntu nilantōyu mirumpiṭit taṭakkiyiṟ
cērntuṭaṉ ceṟinta kuṟaṅkiṟ kuṟaṅkeṉa
mālvari yoḻukiya vāḻi vāḻip
pūveṉap polinta vōti yōti
naḷicciṉi vēṅki nāṇmalar nacci"	(ciṟupāṇ. 19 - 23)
eṉpatūum aṭukkiyatōṟṟa meṉappaṭātōveṉiṉ, paṭātaṉṟē; yāṉik kipōluṅ kuṟaṅku; kuṟaṅkupōlum vāḻiyeṉa aṭukkic collātu kuṟaṅkiṉiyuṭiyāḷeṉṟu tuṇittuk kūṟiya piṉṉark kuṟaṅkeṉa mālvari oḻukiya vāḻi yeṉṟāṉātaliṉeṉpatu. 'tōṟṟa' meṉṟataṉāṉ uvamamum poruḷumāka niṟīi uvamavurupu tōṉṟak kūṟuṅkāl aṭukkappaṭuvateṉpatu.	(36)
uvamam niralniṟiyākavum varumāṟu
312.	niraṉiṟuttu amittal niraṉiṟi cuṇṇam
	varinili vitta mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭiyē.
ituvum eytātaṉa variyaṟiyuṭimiyiṟ kūṟiyavaṟṟōṭu iṉamākaliṉ uvamattiṟkāvatoṉ ṟuṇarttutal nutaliṟṟu.
(i-ḷ.) niraṉiṟuttu amittal niraṉiṟi- uvamattiyum niralē niṟuttup poruḷiṉiyum niralē niṟuttu oppumi kūṟiṉ atu niraṉiṟi yeṉappaṭum; cuṇṇam varinili vitta mūṉṟalaṅkaṭiyē- cuṇṇat tiṉi varinta nilimiyāṉ vanta cuṇṇamum aṭimaṟiyum moḻi māṟṟu meṉṉum mūṉṟumallāta iṭattu e-ṟu.
cuṇṇattiṉi varinta nilimiyāṉ vantaṉaveṉavē cuṇṇan tavira mūṉṟuḷavōveṉiṉ, ataṉi (mūvaruḷ) oruvari vilakka mūvarum vantila reṉṟāṟpōlak koḷka. utāraṇam :
"koṭikuvaḷi koṭṭi nucuppuṇkaṇ mēṉi
matipavaḷa mutta mukamvāy muṟuval
piṭipiṇi maññi naṭinōkkuc cāyal 
vaṭiviṉaḷē vañci makaḷ"	
eṉa varum.
niraṉiṟuttaleṉṉātu 'amitta' leṉṟataṉāṉ niraṉiṟiyaṉṟi amittuk koḷvatum uṇṭu; atu,
"kaḷiṟuṅ kantum pōla naḷikaṭaṟ
kūmpuṅ kalaṉun tōṉṟun
tōṉṟaṉ maṟantōr tuṟikeḻu nāṭṭē"	
eṉa varum. piṟavum aṉṉa.
eṉṟārkku, 'niraṉiṟut tamittal niraṉiṟi ēṉi, varinili vitta mūṉṟalaṅ kaṭiyē' eṉṉātu cuṇṇattiṉi varintōtiyatu eṉṉiyeṉiṉ, avi mūṉṟuñ cuṇṇam pōlac cuṇṇañ ceyyap paṭutaliṉ avvāṟṟāṟ citarntu kiṭappa uvamam kūṟiṉ atu poruḷ viḷakkāteṉpatu aṟivittaṟkeṉpatu. eṉṉi? aṭimaṟiyuḷ ōraṭiyuḷ uvamaṅ kūṟi ōraṭiyuṭ poruḷvittāl iṉitu poruḷkoḷḷātu aṭimaṟikkuṅ kālip piṟitupiṟitāka luṭittu. iṉi ōraṭiyuḷ uvamaṅ kūṟip piṉṉar ettuṇiyuñ ceṉṟu poruḷkūṟi moḻimāṟṟik koḷḷa vippiṉ atuvum uvamattāṟ poruḷ tōṉṟātu. cuṇṇat tiṟkum aḵtokkum.
iṉi, ivvōttiṉiṟ kūṟukiṉṟa uvamaṅkaḷuṭ cilavaṟṟiyuñ collatikārattiṉuḷḷuñ ceyyuḷiyaluḷḷuñ collukiṉṟa cila poruḷkaḷiyum vāṅkikkoṇṭu maṟṟavi ceyyuṭkaṇṇē aṇiyā meṉa, ikkālattāciriyar nūl ceytārumuḷar. avi orutaliyākac ceyyuṭku aṇiyeṉṟu ilakkaṇaṅ kūṟappaṭā. eṉṉi? vallār ceyyiṉ aṇiyākiyum allār ceyyiṉ aṇiyaṉṟākiyum varum, tāṅkāṭṭiya ilakkaṇattiṟ citiyāvaḻiyu meṉpatu. eṉṉi?
"2nāyakarkku nāykaḷpō ṉaṭpiṟ piṟaḻātu
kūuyk kuḻāa muṭaṉkoṭku - māypaṭip
paṉṟi yaṉiyar pakivēnta rāṅkavar
ceṉṟevaṉ ceyvar ceru"	
eṉṟavaḻi, nāypōlum naṭpuṭiyar paṭiyāḷareṉpatu viṉiyuvamam; paṉṟiyaṉiyar pakivēntareṉpatu nāykkup pakiyākiya paṉṟipōla veṉpatu.vēṟṟuvēntar pakivarātalāṉ avvuvami vilakkaritu. aṉṟā māyiṉum aḵtaṇiyeṉappaṭātu, uvamamtāṉ uyarnta taṉmi yiṉ. atu kuṟṟamaṉṟōveṉiṉ,
"pērū raṭṭa kaḷḷiṟku
ōril kōyiṟ ṟērumā ṉiṉṉē"	(puṟam. 300)
eṉpatu kuṟṟamaṉṟākaliṉ atuvuṅ kuṟṟamaṉṟeṉappaṭum. iṉip poṉ māli pōlap pūmāliyum polivu ceytaliṉ itu kuṟṟamākātu; mēlatē kuṟṟameṉpatu. aṟṟaṉṟu, iṉṉacollum iṉṉaporuḷu muṭiyaṉa poṉmāliyeṉavum pūmāliyeṉavum variyaṟuttuk kūṟaliṉmiyiṉ ataṉāṉum ataṉik kuṟṟameṉṟu ilakkaṇattār kūṟiṉ nirampātu. allatūum poruḷatikārattuṭ poruṭpakutikaḷel lāñ ceyyuṭku aṇiyākalāṉ, avi pāṭaluṭ payiṉṟavi yeṉap paṭṭaṉa eṉṟataṉāṉ aviyellān tokuttu aṇiyeṉak kūṟātu vēṟu cilavaṟṟi varintu aṇiyeṉak kūṟutal payamil kūṟṟā meṉpatu.
iṉi, iraṇṭu poruḷeṇṇi, avaṟṟi viṉippaṭukkuṅkāl oruṅkeṉpatoru coṟpeytal ceyyuṭku aṇiyeṉpa. piṟavum iṉṉōraṉṉa palavuñ ceyyuṭ kaṇiyāmeṉpatu avar karuttu. oruṅku yeṉpatēyaṉṟi mūṉṟu tāḻiciyuḷ mūṉṟu poruḷ kūṟi 'eṉavāṅku' eṉpatoru collāṉ muṭintavaḻi 'eṉavāṅku' eṉpatoru moḻi, 'eṉaveṉpatu ōralaṅkāra' meṉalum vēṇṭumākalāṉ avvāṟu vari yaṟuttuk kūṟalamiyāteṉpatu. piṟavum aṉṉa.
iṉi, avaṟṟip poruḷuṟuppeṉpatallatu aṇiyeṉpavāyiṟ, cāttaṉiyuñ cāttaṉāṉaṇiyappaṭṭa muṭiyuntoṭiyum mutalāyavaṟṟi yum vēṟu kaṇṭāṟpōla avvaṇiyuñ ceyyuḷiṉ vēṟākal vēṇṭu meṉpatu.
iṉic, ceyyuṭkaṇi ceyyum poruṭpaṭi ellāṅ kūṟātu cilavē kūṟi oḻiyiṉ atu kuṉṟakkūṟalā (663) meṉpatu. avi yāvi yeṉiṉ,
"akaṉamar kēḷva ṉakaṟci tīrttaṟku
makaṉoṭu pukunta makavunili yeṉāa
maṟukkuṅ kāli maṟutturi moḻiyātu
kuṟippuvēṟu koḷīiya kuṟippunili yeṉāap
pulavik kaṇṇum pōkkiṉ kaṇṇu
maḻutalu maḻāa tuyaṅkalu meṉṟāṅ
kiruvakip paṭṭa maṅkala meṉāap
pulampuṟu kāli yaṟivoṭu paṭātu
pulampukoḷa vanta ceyviṉi yeṉāa
viṉṉō raṉṉa palporuṭ pakuti
naṉṉeṟip pulavar nāṭṭalvaki yuṭiya"	
eṉṟoru cūttirañ ceyyiṉ aviyum alaṅkārameṉap paṭumeṉpatu. avaṟṟukku utāraṇam :
"ākat toṭukkiya putalvaṉ puṉṟalit
tūnīr payanta tuṇiyami piṇiyal"	(akam. 5)
eṉpatu, makavunili.
"ōvac ceytiyi ṉeṉṟuniṉin toṟṟi"	(akam. 5)
eṉpatu, kuṟippunili; eṉṉi? talimakaṉ pōkkiṉi uvakkuṅ kuṟip pallāta kuṟippākaliṉ aḵtaṇiyeṉappaṭum.
"tummuc ceṟuppa vaḻutāḷ"	(kuṟaḷ. 1318)
eṉpatu. pulaviyuḷaḻuta maṅkalam.
"pāvi māytta paṉinīr nōkkamoṭu
. . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . . .
mōyiṉa ḷuyirtta kāli''	(akam. 5)
eṉpatu, pōkkiṉkaṇ aḻāta maṅkalanili.
"viḷinili kēḷā ṭamiyaṇ meṉmela
nalamiku cēvaṭi nilamvaṭuk koḷāak
kuṟuka vantutaṉ kūreyiṟu tōṉṟa"	(akam. 5)
eṉpatu, aṟivoṭu paṭātu pulampukoḷa vanta ceyviṉi; eṉṉi? kēḷātu kēṭṭāḷpōl vantamiyiṉ. atuvum alaṅkārameṉappaṭu meṉṟu cūttirañ ceytukoḷḷal vēṇṭum, ceyyuṭku aṇi vēṟu kūṟiṉeṉpatu.
iṉi, iṅṅaṉaṅ kūṟiṉavellāṅ kuṟṟameṉṟu koḷḷappaṭā; eṉṉi? vēṟu kāraṇamuṇarappeṟātu piṟaḻntu iṭiyaṟavupaṭṭa kāli iṭarppaṭṭuc ceytaṉavātalāṉ, aviyum avvāṟṟāṉamiyu meṉpatu.
iṉi āṉantavuvami yeṉpaṉa cila kuṟṟam akattiyaṉār ceytā reṉak kūṟupavākaliṉ aviyiṟṟi evvāṟu kōṭumeṉiṉ, avitām akattuḷḷum piṟacāṉṟōr ceyyuḷuḷḷum varutaliṟ kuṟṟamākā. akattiyaṉārāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa mūṉṟu tamiḻiṉum aṭaṅkāmi vēṟu āṉantavōtteṉpatu oṉṟu ceytārāyiṉ, akattiyamun tol kāppiyamum nūlāka vanta cāṉṟōr ceyyuḷ kuṟṟam vēṟupaṭāveṉpatu. aḵtēṟ, ceyyuṭku ivi yaṇiyeṉpatūum ivi kuṟṟameṉpatūum mūṉṟatikārattuḷḷum ivvāciriyar yāṇṭuṅ kūṟārōveṉiṉ, cey yuḷiyalil muppattunāṉ kuṟuppuk kūṟi, avaṟṟatiyipu nallicip pulavar ceyyuḷuṟuppeṉavum avi toṭarnta toṭarnili eṇvakiyāṉ toṭarnilic ceyyuṭku vaṉappeṉavum pakuttu, ōtiya ilakkaṇattiṟ piṟaḻntavi ellāṅ kuṟṟameṉpatūum koḷḷavittāṉeṉpatu.	(37)
uvamaviyaṟkup pērāciriyaruri muṟṟiṟṟu.
